- **Critical — rider vehicle-photo uploads were failing with a raw constraint-violation error, unusable end-to-end.** Migration `20260916000000` added `vehicle_photo_front`/`vehicle_photo_side`/`vehicle_photo_rear` as required rider documents and bumped the completeness-count function accordingly, but never updated the CHECK constraint on `delivery_partner_verification_documents.doc_type`, which still only allowed the original 7 values. Every attempt by a rider to save a vehicle photo hit `delivery_partner_verification_documents_doc_type_check` and surfaced as an "upload failed" alert with no useful detail. **Fixed** via migration `20260918000000_delivery_partner_vehicle_photos_doc_type_constraint.sql`, adding the 3 missing values to the constraint. Verified live: `pg_get_constraintdef` now lists all 10 doc types. Checked the shopkeeper app's equivalent table (`store_verification_documents`) for the same class of bug — its CHECK constraint already matches its app's 7 doc types exactly, and the store's separate gallery-photo feature (`store_images`) uses its own table with no doc-type enum at all, so it was never exposed to this bug in the first place.
- Applied the new `billing_info` migration directly via `supabase db query --linked -f <file>` rather than `supabase db push`, after discovering `db push`/`migration list` show every historical migration as "unapplied" remotely (a pre-existing local↔remote migration-history bookkeeping mismatch, unrelated to anything changed this session) — `db push` tried to replay the entire history from scratch and failed immediately on an old, unrelated migration. Not fixed (out of scope, needs `supabase migration repair` run deliberately by whoever manages the project) — flagging so it isn't mistaken for a new regression.

### Store-owner app (`near-now-store_owner`)

- **"Required documents" count showing 7, not including the store's gallery images — confirmed not a bug.** `store_images` (gallery photos, max 5, customer-facing) and `store_verification_documents` (the 7 ID documents) are intentionally separate concepts everywhere in the codebase — the admin panel's document review, the DB's `mark_verification_submitted_if_ready()` completeness function, and both mobile screens' own progress counters only ever look at the 7 verification documents. No code anywhere treats gallery-image count as a gating/required criterion for admin approval. If store photos should become a real approval requirement, that needs new logic (e.g. a `MIN_STORE_IMAGES` check) — nothing currently does this.
- **Owner profile photo uploaded from the Billing Info screen never appeared on the Details screen (one-way sync gap).** Details screen (`store-owner-signup.tsx`) sources its avatar from an `AsyncStorage` key (`owner_profile_image_url`) rather than a network fetch; Billing Info (`billing-info.tsx`) updated the database and its own local state on upload but never wrote that same `AsyncStorage` key, so Details kept showing the old/blank photo until something else happened to rewrite it. The reverse direction (Details → Billing Info) already worked, since Billing Info does a genuine network refetch on every mount. **Fixed** by exporting a shared `OWNER_IMAGE_KEY` constant from `lib/storage.ts` (both screens now import the same key instead of Details defining its own local copy) and having `billing-info.tsx` write it via `AsyncStorage.setItem` right after a successful upload.
- **Store name/address (and, transitively, the Billing page's owner name) intermittently showing stale or blank data that never self-corrected.** Root cause: `fetchStoresCached()` in `lib/appCache.ts` returns the same in-memory cached array unconditionally whenever it's still within its 10-minute TTL — including calls whose entire purpose was "refresh in the background after painting from cache." `home.tsx`, `profile.tsx`, and `billing-info.tsx` all had this exact pattern (paint from `peekStores()`, then call `fetchStoresCached()` again "to refresh," which was actually a no-op while warm). If the cache was ever seeded with a wrong/stale store row, nothing could correct it for up to 10 minutes, and a stale/wrong cached store id could also make `billing-info.tsx`'s billing lookup silently 403 (wrong store, fails the owner-id check server-side) leaving the owner name blank. Confirmed via a direct database query that the underlying data was always correct (`stores.name`, `app_users.name` both populated correctly) — this was purely a client caching bug, not missing data. **Fixed** by adding a real `forceFetchStores()` (always hits the network, ignores the warm-cache short-circuit) and switching `home.tsx`'s background refresh, `profile.tsx`'s background refresh, and `billing-info.tsx`'s store-id resolution to use it. The Details screen (`store-owner-signup.tsx`) already did its own direct fetch bypassing the cache module entirely (an earlier fix this session), so it was unaffected once written that way.
- Shopkeeper's Details (view-only) screen had a redundant "Your Details" header rendered *above* the 4-button verification nav bar, plus the whole screen was missing horizontal/top padding (the removed header's own padding had been the only thing providing it) — together read as visibly misaligned. **Fixed** by removing the duplicate header and giving the screen its own properly-padded content style.
- Shopkeeper's Details page didn't visually match the rider app's equivalent page (different avatar size, no title/subtitle under the avatar, fields shown as separate boxes instead of one bordered card, no lock-once-set behavior on the owner photo — unlike the rider page's photo, which now locks after first upload). **Fixed** by rebuilding the view-only render to match the rider page's structure, dimensions (76×76 avatar, matching card/label/input styling), copy ("Your Details" title + same subtitle wording), and by adding the same lock-after-first-upload guard to the owner-photo picker.
- Removed the redundant "Add Billing Info" alert button on the Documents screen's post-submit alert and the separate "Billing Info" card on the Status (pending-verification) screen — both were superseded by the 4-tab verification nav bar's own "Billing" tab, added earlier this session, making a second/third entry point to the same screen redundant clutter.

### Rider app (`NAT_Near-Now_Rider-`)

- Riders previously had **no way at all** to set a profile photo during the pre-approval verification window — the only photo-editing UI (`(tabs)/profile.tsx`) is gated behind full account approval. **Fixed**: added a photo picker (upload + lock-after-first-set, mirroring the store owner's) to both the Details screen (`signup.tsx`) and the new Billing Info screen, both backed by the same existing `profile_image_url` column/`delivery_partner_image` bucket/`/delivery-partner/photo-urls` endpoint — no new backend surface needed for this part.
- Removed the same redundant "Add Billing Info" alert button and Status-screen card as the store-owner app, for the same reason (the nav bar's Billing tab already covers it).

## 2026-07-29: Dependency cleanup, vulnerability audit, and build/lint fixes

Not a feature session — a clean-reinstall + `npm audit fix` + unused-dependency sweep across all 7 areas (`root`, `frontend`, `backend`, `admin`, `nearandnowcustomerapp`, `NAT_Near-Now_Rider-`, `near-now-store_owner`), plus whatever regressions/pre-existing bugs surfaced while verifying it didn't break anything. Every removed package was confirmed unused via `depcheck` **and** a manual repo-wide grep (including `app.config.js`/`app.json` plugin lists and native config-plugin files for the mobile apps) before deletion — nothing was removed on `depcheck`'s word alone. Full verification per web folder: `npm ci` (lockfile-consistency), `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build`, the existing test suites, and a live headless-browser smoke test of the frontend + admin dev servers plus a real `curl` against the backend (`/api/products/categories` returned real Supabase data) — zero console errors, zero regressions found in application behavior itself. Two real, pre-existing bugs were caught during this verification pass (see below), and vulnerability counts were reduced across the board; the specific package-count/version-bump numbers aren't repeated here since they're a moving target and not what this doc tracks — see the session's own summary for exact figures if needed.

- **`frontend/vite.config.ts`'s `manualChunks.admin` block broke every production build (`npm run build`/`vite build` failed outright with "Could not resolve entry module").** Found 2026-07-29 while re-verifying `npm run build` after the dependency cleanup — confirmed pre-existing and unrelated to the cleanup itself: the block listed 4 files under `src/pages/admin/` (`AdminDashboardPage.tsx`, `ProductsPage.tsx`, `OrdersPage.tsx`, `CustomersPage.tsx`), a directory that no longer exists, evidently left behind by the 2026-07-31 "duplicate admin panel in customer frontend" removal (Fixed Issues, Website frontend → Low, `adminService.ts` trim) — that fix deleted the pages but never touched this now-dangling `vite.config.ts` reference. This wasn't previously caught because the existing fix around it (Fixed Issues, Website frontend → Low, `sanitize.ts`/`csrf.ts`/`dompurify`/`crypto-js` removal) explicitly used **"a temporary local workaround for the pre-existing, unrelated broken `admin` manualChunk entry"** to get its own verification build to succeed, rather than fixing the underlying config — see Build/Test Status below. **Fixed 2026-07-29** by deleting the dead `admin` manualChunks entry outright (the pages are gone, not renamed — nothing to repoint it to). Verified via a real `npm run build`, which now succeeds end-to-end.
- **`eslint-plugin-react` was never actually installed** — see Website frontend → Low (new) in Open Issues above for the full write-up (fixed the same day, along with 2 of the 141 lint problems it surfaced: a bare empty `catch {}` in `services/supabase.ts` and a `let`-that-should-be-`const` in `utils/apiBase.ts`). Listed here too since it's as much a "found while verifying the dependency cleanup" bug as the `vite.config.ts` one above.
- **`backend` has never declared its own dependency on `razorpay`, despite 9 files importing it** — see Backend → Low in Open Issues above for the full write-up. Found the same way (depcheck flagged `razorpay` as unused in root, which led to checking who actually uses it). **Not fixed** — flagged as a latent risk, not touched.
- **Vulnerabilities knowingly left open, by explicit decision, rather than force-fixed:** `react-router`'s remaining CVEs (open redirect, SSR-hydration constructor injection) are only patched at v8.3.0 across every consumer in this repo (`frontend` on v6, `admin` on v6, root on v7) — fixing fully means a breaking major-version migration touching 25+ files in `frontend` alone, decided against for now in favor of a dedicated, separately-tested migration later. Similarly, the bulk of the 3 mobile apps' remaining vulnerabilities (Expo/React-Native/Babel/Jest/Metro toolchain, `sharp`) only resolve via a breaking Expo SDK bump requiring a real native rebuild + device testing to verify — left open for the same reason; almost all of that exposure is build-time tooling, not code shipped to a user's device. `near-now-store_owner`'s `expo-camera` package was also deliberately left installed despite zero JS usage, since `plugins/withTabletSupport.js` has a hardcoded Android-manifest patch (`tools:replace`) targeting a barcode-scanner activity that only exists because `expo-camera`'s native module pulls it in — removing the package without also touching that patch risks an unverifiable Gradle manifest-merge failure.

## 2026-07-29 (same day, new feature build): Custom-product admin-review gate + admin activity visibility

Three linked pieces of work in one session: (1) a data-curation pass flagging fresh produce as loose-sellable and deduping `master_products`, (2) closing a real, live tax-compliance bug in the shopkeeper app's "Add Custom Product" feature by adding the admin-review gate it never had, and (3) a broader admin-activity-visibility feature (who-approved-what, a unified activity log, cross-admin notifications) built on top of the same review pattern. Every live-DB change below was applied via `supabase db query --linked -f <file>` (not `db push`, for the same pre-existing local↔remote migration-history mismatch documented in the 2026-07-28 Billing Info entry above) and verified against the real database — every test row created for verification (fake shopkeeper/admin sessions, temporary `admin`/`manager`/`viewer` test accounts, test product submissions) was deleted afterward and confirmed gone via a fresh count query, not just assumed.

### Data curation (`master_products`)

- Investigated `is_loose` flag coverage on request. Found exactly 32 rows flagged `is_loose = true` (not the 39 the user initially expected — verified 3 independent ways: exact `is_loose = true` count, `name ILIKE 'Loose%'` count, and cross-checking against a user-pasted CSV export, which turned out to be an unrelated, unfiltered catalog dump rather than evidence of 39).
- Identified 1,067 rows (272 distinct product names) across `Fruits`/`Vegetables` categories that were unbranded fresh produce sellable loose but not flagged — the `brand` column turned out to be an unreliable signal (auto-populated for nearly everything, either a `"Fresh"` placeholder or the first word of the name), so category was used instead. Excluded 12 miscategorized pooja/floral items (garlands, loose flowers) after user confirmation, then flagged the remaining 1,044 rows (301 distinct names) `is_loose = true` via migration `20260922000000_flag_fresh_produce_as_loose.sql`.
- Found and fixed a duplicate-row problem in the same catalog slice: repeated store-sync imports had left up to 8 copies of the same product name as separate `master_products` rows. Deduped all 1,076 then-loose rows down to 303 distinct products (773 rows removed) via migration `20260922000001_dedupe_loose_master_products.sql`, keeping the earliest-`created_at` row per name. Verified zero of the removed rows were referenced by any store's `products.master_product_id` (which is `ON DELETE RESTRICT`) before deleting, so nothing broke.

### Custom-product admin-review gate (`product_submissions`)

- **Critical, live tax-compliance bug — every shopkeeper-submitted custom product silently got 0% GST charged on invoices.** The mobile app's "Add Custom Product" screens (`near-now-store_owner/app/add.product.tsx` and the `(tabs)/stock.tsx` inline form) never collected `hsn_code`/`gst_rate`, and inserted directly into the shared `master_products` catalog from the client via a Supabase RLS policy scoped only to "any live shopkeeper session" — no admin review, no per-store scoping, and no validation of what was actually being added. The resulting `gst_rate = NULL` was silently coerced to 0% by `invoice.service.ts`'s `info?.gst_rate ?? 0`, so every custom product shipped with incorrect (missing) tax on real customer invoices. **Fixed** by replacing the direct-insert path with a staging table (`product_submissions`, migration `20260923000000_product_submissions_admin_review.sql`) — the shopkeeper app now submits there instead (new `POST/GET /shopkeeper/product-submissions`, `lib/storeProducts.ts`'s `addCustomMasterProduct` rewritten to call the backend instead of `supabase.from('master_products').insert(...)`), and a row-locked SQL function (`review_product_submission`, mirroring this repo's existing `review_store_profile_change_request` pattern) only promotes it into `master_products` + the submitting store's `products` once an admin supplies `hsn_code`/`gst_rate` at approval. The old `shopkeeper_can_add_master_product` RLS policy was dropped; confirmed live via a real anon-key+shopkeeper-token `curl` that the old direct-write path now 401s with `new row violates row-level security policy`.
- New admin panel page (`ProductSubmissionsPage.tsx`, Catalog → Products → Product Submissions) to review/approve/reject submissions, plus a full field-level **Edit** action (name, category, brand, description, image, prices, unit, loose flag, quantities, and — per explicit follow-up request — HSN code/description/GST/CGST/SGST too) so an admin can correct anything the shopkeeper submitted before it reaches the catalog, not just the tax fields.
- **Found and fixed during self-verification, before this ever reached the user:** the approve endpoint originally re-required `hsn_code`/`gst_rate` in the *request body* even when an admin had already saved them via Edit on a prior action — caught by testing the exact "edit now, approve later without resending" flow, not by a bug report. Fixed via migration `20260923000002_review_product_submission_hsn_gst_fallback.sql`, making the SQL function `COALESCE` the request's values with whatever's already stored on the row.
- Shopkeeper-facing: a new "My Submissions" status screen (`app/product-submissions.tsx`) and an app-wide blocking outcome modal on approval/rejection — generalized from the pre-existing store-profile-change-only `useProfileChangeOutcomeGate`/`ProfileChangeOutcomeModal` into `useReviewOutcomeGate`/`ReviewOutcomeModal` (same polling mechanism, now handles both notification kinds; the old profile-change-only files were deleted rather than left as unused duplicates).

### Admin activity visibility

- Per explicit requirement: store/rider profile-change-request review pages now show who reviewed a request and when (`reviewed_by_name`/`reviewed_by_role`, resolved via a second query since `reviewed_by` has no FK to `admins` for PostgREST to auto-embed), and access to those two pages is now restricted to `admin`+`super_admin` only — a new dedicated `profile_change_requests.view`/`.edit` permission replaces the broader `store_verification.*`/`delivery_partners.*` ones they previously shared with `manager`/`viewer`, who lose access to these two specific pages as a result (deliberate, per explicit instruction — verified live: a real `manager`-role test session now gets `403 Missing permission: profile_change_requests.view` on both).
- New unified **Activity Log** page (`/activity-log`, all four roles) aggregating every review action across all 5 places an admin approves/rejects something (store/rider profile changes, product submissions, store/rider verification documents) — reads the same source-of-truth tables those pages already show rather than adding a 6th table every controller would have to remember to also write to. Visibility is role-scoped server-side (`adminActivityLog.controller.ts`): `super_admin` sees every row including other super admins'; `admin`/`manager`/`viewer` see admin-tier actions only (their own + other admins'/managers'/viewers') but not super-admin-only ones; `viewer` additionally gets the detail/rejection-reason field stripped to `null`. All three rules verified live with temporary `admin`/`manager`/`viewer` test accounts.
- Extended the existing `admin_notifications` table (already shared across all admins since a prior fix, migration `20260827000001`) so every one of the 5 review actions above also broadcasts a notification other admins can see, not just the shopkeeper/rider whose request it was — new `notifyAdminsOfReviewAction()` in `notification.service.ts`, called from all 5 review controllers. Added `actor_id`/`actor_role` columns and a `current_admin_role()` helper (migration `20260924000000_admin_activity_visibility.sql`) so the table's RLS policy can apply the same super-admin-exclusion rule as the Activity Log — verified live via direct anon-key+`x-admin-token` REST calls (mimicking exactly how `NotificationsPage.tsx` queries it) that a `super_admin`-authored notification is invisible to `admin`/`manager`/`viewer` sessions while an `admin`-authored one is visible to everyone including `super_admin`.
- **Informational, not a bug:** the Activity Log endpoint queries all 5 source tables in full (no pagination) on every request. Fine at current data volume (verified against real historical rows); worth revisiting if any of the 5 tables grows large enough to make that slow.

## Reference: Pricing Model / GST

Confirmed by product owner, 2026-07-16. Two independent, stacking GST layers — needed context for anything touching prices, checkout totals, or invoices:

1. **Per-item GST**, baked into each product's sellable price: `item_price = master_products.discounted_price + (discounted_price × master_products.gst_rate / 100)`. `discounted_price` is the pre-tax base. `is_loose = true` (loose/weighed products) → `gst_rate` is always 0. `is_loose = false` (packaged products) → follow the `gst_rate` column. Reference implementation: `frontend/src/utils/priceGst.ts`, mirrored in `admin/src/utils/priceGst.ts` and `frontend/src/services/supabase.ts:206-221`.
2. **A separate flat 5% GST on the whole bill at checkout**, mandated by GoI as a business-level tax (the rate can change if GoI changes it) — applied on top of the already-GST-inclusive item subtotal. This is intentional, not double taxation (see Confirmed Not Bugs below). Implemented in `frontend/src/utils/checkoutCalculations.ts`.

Any server-side price/total recomputation must replicate both layers or it will silently diverge from what the customer actually saw and agreed to pay.

## Confirmed Not Bugs

- **Website double-charges GST on every order** — the flat 5% added at checkout on top of GST-inclusive product prices is intentional by-design pricing (see Reference above), confirmed by the product owner. Not double-taxation.
- **Checkout email field is HTML-`required`** — originally flagged as blocking OTP-only customers with no real email from completing checkout. Briefly "fixed" (attribute removed) on 2026-07-30, then reverted the same day: confirmed by the product owner that a valid email is a required field at checkout, by design, not a bug. `required` attribute restored.
- **Rider app's OTP-verification-ticket threading** — traced end-to-end against the backend's HMAC ticket verification, holds up (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Rider app's "remove already-taken orders instantly" fix** — no wrong-order-removal or stale-closure race found (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Customer app's order-placement routing through the backend** — matches the documented Fixed description, no regression (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Store-owner app's cache-clear-on-suspend fix** — a correctly-scoped, independent fix, not a duplicate or regression of the documented suspend-on-edit stale-cache bug (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Shopkeeper verification rebuild — flat-vs-grouped dual data structure** in `upload-documents.tsx` — no drift risk (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Shopkeeper verification rebuild — image-upload guard clauses** — bail out before any spinner/optimistic state, no dangling UI (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Shopkeeper verification rebuild — migration's `storage.objects` regex renames** — safe, `storeId` is a UUID and can't collide with the literal doc-type substrings (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Shopkeeper verification rebuild — backend's `isDocType` type-guard coverage** — no reachable path where an unvalidated `docType` hits `DOC_LABELS` (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Shopkeeper verification rebuild — admin's `documents.find()` lookup** — backend always returns all 7 `DOC_TYPES`, so it can never fail to find a sibling row (checked 2026-07-18).
- **Admin panel — backend-API `fetch()` calls needing admin auth** all correctly attach the `Authorization` header (checked during the admin-panel audit pass).
- **Admin panel — `.from()` calls in pages/services** all correctly use `getAdminClient()` (aside from the already-documented pre-login fallback).
- **Admin panel — `NotificationProvider` and `ErrorBoundary`** are both correctly mounted.
- **Store-owner app's `setAllProductsOffline`/`restoreActiveProductsOnline` no-ops** — re-investigated 2026-07-26. These two functions genuinely do nothing, but the "Go Offline" toggle's actual product-hiding mechanism is a separate, working one: `app/(tabs)/home.tsx`'s `toggleOnline` also `PATCH`es `stores.is_active`, and the customer website's location-based browsing path (`ShopPage.tsx`/`HomePage.tsx`, the common case) calls the `get_nearby_store_ids` RPC, which filters `WHERE is_active = true` on `stores` — confirmed live via `pg_get_functiondef`. So a store going offline does correctly disappear from the primary nearby-browsing flow; the no-op functions are vestigial, not the load-bearing mechanism their names/comments implied. The investigation did surface a real, related gap in the *no-location* browsing path — see Website frontend → High (fixed 2026-07-26).

## Build/Test Status

- **Backend:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vitest` 17/17 passing. Two `payment.service.test.ts` env-isolation/mock bugs were fixed (dotenv `override: true` clobbering test-set env vars in `NODE_ENV=test`, and a `vi.stubGlobal('fetch', ...)` mock shape issue) — both were test-only bugs, not product bugs.
- **Frontend (website):** `tsc --noEmit` fully clean (2026-07-30 — the long-standing `ProtectedRoute.tsx` `FallbackComponent` type error is fixed: it was typing `ErrorFallback`'s props by hand instead of importing `react-error-boundary`'s own `FallbackProps`, whose `error` is `unknown`, not `Error`; the file is still dead code, unimported anywhere, but no longer fails the build). Test suite 26/26. `npm run build` now succeeds end-to-end (2026-07-29 — `vite.config.ts`'s dangling `admin` manualChunk entry, previously worked around locally rather than fixed, is now actually removed; see Fixed Issues, 2026-07-29 section). `npm run lint` now actually runs (2026-07-29 — `eslint-plugin-react` was missing, see same section) and currently reports 139 pre-existing problems (54 errors, 85 warnings), 2 fixed same day; the rest is real, sizeable, deliberately-not-mass-fixed lint debt, not a new regression.
- **Admin panel:** `tsc --noEmit` clean. **No test infrastructure exists** — zero automated coverage on the app that gates every store/rider approval and holds admin auth.
- **Store-owner app:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; Jest suite 21/21.
- **Rider app:** `tsc --noEmit` clean. `expo lint` — **0 problems**, as of 2026-07-31, down from the original 23/4 baseline across four passes: deleting dead `lib/authService.ts`/`components/MapViewWrapper.tsx` (and the `react-native-maps` dependency/Google Maps config it dragged along) → 20/2; fixing 2 `no-unescaped-entities` errors, 2 `array-type` warnings, and 1 `no-require-imports` warning (scoped lint-suppress, since that `require()` is a deliberate Expo-Go-compatibility guard) → 15/0; removing `documents.tsx`'s dead `saveSession` import and `orders.tsx`'s unnecessary `tab` dependency → 13/0; resolving all 13 remaining `exhaustive-deps` "missing dependency" warnings individually → 0/0. **No test infrastructure exists.**
- **Customer app:** `tsc --noEmit` clean after fixing a CJS/ESM interop issue in a platform-switch shim and a stale local Expo Router type-manifest cache (not a real bug). `expo lint` was failing outright due to a stray, untracked local `src/` directory shadowing the real `app/`/`lib/`/`hooks/` source — local machine cruft, not a repo-wide issue. **No test infrastructure exists.**

## Audit History

- **2026-07-15:** original admin-approval-flow audit (`bug_fixes_2026-07-15.md`), later merged into this doc.
- **2026-07-16:** full-codebase audit across all 7 areas; a later same-day pass cross-checked against `QA_AUDIT.md`/`qa_audit_old.md` (an independent 2026-05/06 QA pass) and added 58 confirmed-still-open findings. A separate scoped pass the same day covered checkout pricing/GST correctness (`docs/gst-calculation-explained.md` created as the GST math reference).
- **2026-07-17:** shopkeeper document-verification feature built from scratch (per-document review workflow, replacing a broken single-JSON-blob design), followed same day by a hardening pass (RLS/grant gaps on the new table, missing tables added to the realtime publication) and a second bug pass on the rebuild.
- **2026-07-18:** verification documents split into front/back (5 → 7 required documents); admin notifications for verification activity added; store/owner image buckets tracked in migrations for the first time. A later same-day pass audited only code changed since the prior sessions (admin dashboard redesign, store-owner revoked-store-redirect fixes).
- **2026-07-23:** deep-dive re-audit spanning all 4 repos/folders, run as 5 parallel passes (backend; admin+frontend; shopkeeper; rider; customer). Followed by five same-day fix passes closing out the highest-severity new findings in priority order (backend/DB-only items first, then the acceptance race, then both mobile apps' session-token storage, then the shopkeeper push-notification wiring, then the delivery-fee business-decision sweep).
- **2026-07-24:** full re-verification pass across every open item in the doc (151 at the time), via parallel review against actual current code rather than trusting the doc's own text. Found and corrected one duplicate bullet, several items already resolved by prior undated work (closing out both long-standing customer-app TOP PRIORITY Critical items), a few partially-fixed items, and two misattributed/misfiled items.
- **2026-07-25 / 2026-07-26:** continued targeted fix passes (several reported directly by the user), covering remaining admin-panel High items, rider background location, the rider payout system, GSTIN/receiver structured fields, and the last customer-app push-notification gap.
- **2026-07-28:** full 6-way deep-dive re-audit (backend, admin, website frontend, shopkeeper app, rider app, customer app), run as 6 parallel passes. Cross-checked all findings against this doc's existing Fixed Issues and Confirmed Not Bugs before filing — none were duplicates or already-resolved. Added 12 new Open Issues: 1 backend Critical (payment-verify replay), 2 admin Critical (approval-permission gap, approval-without-documents gap), 1 website High (split-payment amount validation), 2 store-owner High (owner-photo lock bypass, owner-photo cross-account leak), 1 rider High (verification-gate async race — a direct side effect of the 2026-07-31 cache-seeding fix), 1 rider Medium (Home screen's looser verification check), 2 customer-app Medium (stale fee-info modal copy, coupon not revalidated against `min_order_value`), plus 1 backend Low and 1 rider Low already noted above.
- **2026-07-28 (same day, follow-up fix pass):** closed both Admin panel Critical items found above. (1) Document-approval gate: added client-side `approvalReadiness()` checks to `StoresPage.tsx`/`DeliveryPage.tsx`, verified via `tsc --noEmit` and `vite build`. (2) Role-permission RLS gap: wrote and applied migration `20260919000000_stores_delivery_partners_permission_rls.sql` (extending `admin_has_permission()` RLS to `stores`/`delivery_partners`), applied live via `supabase db query --linked` and verified via `pg_policies`/`has_table_privilege`. That verification surfaced a new, previously-undocumented Critical: `stores` has its own `"Allow all for service role"` policy misscoped to `roles: {public}` (identical bug class to the already-known `app_users` hole), which bypasses the RLS fix just applied and lets any anon-key caller write `stores` directly with no admin session at all. Per explicit instruction, this was **not fixed** — held and documented as a new Admin panel → Critical open item instead, same treatment as the `app_users` instance.
- **2026-07-28 (same day, regression review):** a full regression review of every fix made so far today found and closed 4 real regressions: (1) the `stores`/`delivery_partners` RLS migration broke `DeliveryPage.tsx`'s online/offline toggle for `manager`-role admins — fixed with a new migration (`20260920000000`) splitting the RLS check from a column-aware trigger. (2) `profile.tsx`'s owner-photo reconciliation could revert a freshly-uploaded photo on a stale background refresh — fixed with an `ownerImageUploadedAtRef`/`fetchIssuedAt` guard. (3) The rider verification gate's new `AppState` re-check amplified a pre-existing bug (network blips kicking out already-verified riders) — fixed with a `verifiedRef` guard. (4) A stale "18%" GSTIN line in the customer app's `checkout.tsx`, exposed (not caused) by the `cart.tsx` fix — updated to match the website's already-corrected copy. One item was found real but deliberately left open (rider `home.tsx`'s `isApproved` staleness — a self-documented, currently-hypothetical edge case). Also fixed, per explicit request: the twin `app_users`-class RLS hole on `stores` itself (`"Allow all for service role"` misscoped to `{public}`) — held on discovery, fixed once explicitly authorized, verified live via a real anon-key `curl` before/after.
- **2026-07-28 (same day, frontend/UI-focused deep dive):** a separate 5-way parallel audit (customer website, admin panel, and all three mobile apps), explicitly scoped to frontend/UI-layer bugs rather than backend/security (already covered above) — rendering/state races, loading/error/empty states, form UX, navigation, memory leaks. Cross-checked against this doc's Fixed Issues/Confirmed Not Bugs first; none were duplicates. Added 26 new Open Issues across all 5 areas: website (3 High incl. a loose-item quantity-stepper bug and two stale-fetch races, 2 Medium), admin panel (1 High — new categories vanish from their own list, 2 Medium, 4 Low), store-owner app (2 High incl. an invisible stuck-filter bug and an unconfirmed delete, 1 Medium, 1 Low), rider app (1 High — a silent orphaned photo-upload failure, 4 Medium, plus the 1 pre-existing Low), customer app (2 High incl. a missing ErrorBoundary and a wallet double-debit risk, 4 Medium, 3 Low/grouped-minor). None fixed yet this pass — logged for a follow-up fix session.
- **2026-07-29:** dependency-cleanup and vulnerability-audit pass across all 7 areas (clean reinstall + `npm audit fix` + unused-package removal, every removal verified via `depcheck` plus manual grep before deleting). Full regression verification followed (`npm ci`, `tsc --noEmit`, `vite build`, existing test suites, plus a live headless-browser smoke test of `frontend`/`admin` and a real `curl` against `backend`) — zero application regressions. Surfaced and fixed 2 real pre-existing bugs unrelated to the cleanup itself (a stale `vite.config.ts` manualChunk entry that broke every production build, and a missing `eslint-plugin-react` dependency that had silently disabled linting entirely); the latter's fix then surfaced 141 previously-invisible lint problems, 2 of which (a bare empty `catch` and a `let`-that-should-be-`const`) were fixed the same day, with the remainder logged as new Open Issues rather than mass-fixed. Also newly found and logged (not fixed, by explicit decision): `backend`'s undeclared implicit dependency on `razorpay`, and several vulnerabilities across the web and mobile apps left open because fixing them fully requires a breaking major-version migration (`react-router` to v8, or an Expo SDK bump) that needs its own dedicated, tested pass. See Fixed Issues → "2026-07-29: Dependency cleanup, vulnerability audit, and build/lint fixes" for full detail.
- **2026-07-29 (same day, new feature build):** a `master_products.is_loose` data-curation pass (corrected a user-expected count of 39 down to the real 32, flagged 1,044 previously-unflagged fresh-produce rows as loose, then deduped 773 repeated-import duplicate rows) followed by two linked feature builds. First, closed a real, live tax-compliance bug: the shopkeeper app's "Add Custom Product" feature wrote straight into the shared `master_products` catalog with zero admin review and no HSN/GST fields collected, silently shipping every custom product at 0% GST on invoices — replaced with a `product_submissions` staging table + admin-review gate (approve/reject, full field-level edit including tax fields, HSN/GST required only at final approval with a fallback to whatever was already saved via Edit — a gap caught during this session's own verification, not by a bug report). Second, built an admin-activity-visibility feature per explicit requirement: store/rider profile-change pages now show who reviewed a request and restrict access to `admin`+`super_admin` only (new `profile_change_requests.*` permission, `manager`/`viewer` lose access to just these two pages); a new unified Activity Log page aggregates all 5 review flows (store/rider profile changes, product submissions, store/rider verification docs) with role-scoped visibility (`super_admin` sees everything, everyone else sees admin-tier actions only, `viewer` gets a detail-stripped view); and the existing shared `admin_notifications` table now broadcasts every review action to other admins under the same visibility rule. All role-based visibility rules (permission gating, Activity Log filtering, `admin_notifications` RLS) verified live against temporary `admin`/`manager`/`viewer` test accounts and sessions, all cleaned up afterward. See Fixed Issues → "2026-07-29 (same day, new feature build): Custom-product admin-review gate + admin activity visibility" for full detail.
- **2026-07-29 (same day, closed the last open Backend Critical):** fixed the payment `/verify` replay bug found 2026-07-28 (Backend → Critical, above) — added the missing `payment.notes.internal_order_id` check, `requireCustomer` auth, and an order-ownership check to `/create`/`/verify`; updated the website's `paymentGateway.ts` (previously sent no auth header at all) to attach it via the existing `getAuthHeaders()` helper. Verified live against real orders with a temporary session token (401 unauthenticated, 403 cross-customer, 200 for a genuine own-order request); `vitest` 17/17 still passing. Moved from Open Issues to Fixed Issues → Backend → Critical. This was the only Backend Critical item and the only Critical item outside Supabase schema — Backend's Open Issues now has no Critical section at all, down to 2 Low only.
- **2026-07-29 (same day, closed the Admin panel High item):** fixed the Categories-list bug found 2026-07-28 (Admin panel → High, above) — `CategoriesPage.tsx`'s `filteredCategories`/`stats.totalCategories` no longer hard-filter out 0-product categories, which had made every newly-created category vanish immediately with no way back to edit/delete it. Verified `tsc --noEmit` clean and, live against the real database, that this was actively affecting 5 real categories (`Body Creams & Lotions`, `Intimate Hygiene`, `Nail Care`, `Beauty & Makeup`, `Sexual Wellness`), all confirmed to have 0 products. Moved from Open Issues to Fixed Issues → Admin panel → High. Open Issues total now 39 (was 41 before today's two fixes): 2 Critical, 6 High, 13 Medium, 18 Low.
- **2026-07-29 (same day, closed both Supabase Critical items):** fixed the `app_users` RLS hole (full anon read/write, including `session_token` — same misconfigured-policy-scope bug class already fixed on `stores` 2026-07-28) and its entangled direct consequence, the website's `updateCustomerProfile` writing straight to `app_users`/`customers` from the browser. Full repo grep across every app found no other legitimate anon-key dependency on this table except one dead-code read path and one non-`SECURITY DEFINER` trigger (`fill_product_store_info`) that would have silently broken the shopkeeper app's "add product to store" flow — fixed in the same migration by making it `SECURITY DEFINER`. `updateCustomerProfile` now routes through the backend's existing `PATCH /api/customers/me` instead of writing directly; `email` dropped from its accepted fields as confirmed-dead (a separate OTP-verified flow already owns email changes). Verified live end-to-end: anon-key read/write against `app_users` both now correctly return nothing/affect nothing (previously a full customer-data leak and an unauthenticated write); the new customer-profile path and the shopkeeper trigger both re-verified still working correctly post-fix. `tsc --noEmit` clean on `frontend`/`backend`; 26/26 and 17/17 test suites still passing. **Open Issues total now 37** (was 41 at the start of today's session): 0 Critical, 6 High, 13 Medium, 18 Low — no Critical items remain anywhere in the codebase.
- **2026-07-29 (same day, closed 2 of 6 remaining High items — user picked these two):** fixed the split cash/UPI payment validation gap (Website frontend) and the wallet double-tap double-debit risk (Customer app), both found 2026-07-28. The split fix uncovered a bigger root cause than originally scoped: the backend's `placeCheckoutSchema` (Zod) never declared `split_cash_amount`/`split_upi_amount`, so `z.object()`'s default key-stripping behavior (confirmed via a direct Node test) silently discarded them before the controller ever saw them — the split-payment feature had no server-side record of its split amounts at all, confirmed via a live DB query finding zero orders with split data anywhere in `notes`. Fixed both the schema gap and the actual sum-vs-total validation (client in `CheckoutPage.tsx`, server in `placeCheckoutOrder`). The wallet fix added a local `inFlight`-style ref guard to both wallet-retry call sites (`app/orders.tsx`, `app/order/[id].tsx`), mirroring `usePaymentFlow`'s own ref that only ever covered its Razorpay path. `tsc --noEmit` clean across `frontend`/`backend`/customer app; 26/26 and 17/17 test suites still passing. **Open Issues total now 35**: 0 Critical, 4 High, 13 Medium, 18 Low.
- **2026-07-29 (same day, closed 2 more High items — user picked these two of the remaining 4):** fixed `CheckoutPage.tsx`'s Order Summary quantity stepper (hardcoded whole-number step regardless of `item.isLoose`, silently 3x-ing a 0.5kg loose item to 1.5kg on one `+` tap, and deleting a 0.75kg item outright on `-` instead of stepping down) by mirroring `CartItem.tsx`'s already-correct 0.25kg-increment/floor logic inline. Also fixed the stale-fetch races on `ProductDetailPage.tsx` (cancelled-flag guard, same pattern as `SearchPage.tsx`'s existing fix, plus resetting the quantity stepper on `productId` change) and `CategoryPage.tsx` (a sequence-ref guard instead, since its `fetchProducts` is a memoized callback also invoked directly by the "Try again" button, not just the effect). `tsc --noEmit` clean; existing 26/26 frontend suite still passing (no dedicated tests added for either — neither was click-tested live in a browser). **Open Issues total now 33**: 0 Critical, 2 High, 13 Medium, 18 Low. Both remaining High items are in the mobile apps: the rider app's silent orphaned billing-photo upload, and the customer app's missing `ErrorBoundary`.
- **2026-07-29 (same day, closed the last 2 High items — zero High severity remaining anywhere):** fixed the rider app's `billing-info.tsx` silent orphaned photo upload (wrapped the previously-uncaught `PATCH /delivery-partner/photo-urls` follow-up call in its own try/catch with an explicit alert, mirroring `profile.tsx`'s already-correct pattern) and the customer app's missing `ErrorBoundary` (ported the rider app's component verbatim, adapted to this app's own color constants, mounted at the outermost level of `_layout.tsx`). While fixing the latter, the user separately reported the customer app's OTP auto-read "isn't working perfectly" and asked to look at how the shopkeeper/rider apps implement it — this turned out to be exactly the already-documented Medium item below (no synchronous double-submit lock; discards all but the last digit on autofill/paste; missing `textContentType`/`autoComplete` autofill-hint props) — fixed by porting the shopkeeper/rider apps' proven-working implementation verbatim (including removing `maxLength={1}`, which neither reference implementation sets, since it risks clipping an autofilled multi-character delivery before the new distribution logic runs). `tsc --noEmit` clean across the rider and customer apps; `expo lint` 0 problems on the rider app (unchanged baseline), and confirmed not runnable at all on the customer app due to a pre-existing, unrelated tooling misconfiguration (already noted on 2 earlier customer-app fixes) rather than anything touched here. **Open Issues total now 30**: 0 Critical, 0 High, 12 Medium, 18 Low. No High or Critical severity issues remain anywhere in the codebase.
- **2026-08-04:** deep-dive re-audit triggered by reviewing Rounak Jana's commit `64cf747` ("Require store approval for customer-facing product and nearby-store visibility," 2026-08-03), run as 4 parallel passes (`near-and-now` backend+frontend+admin+schema; store-owner app; customer app; rider app), cross-checked against this doc's existing Open/Fixed Issues before filing. The audited commit itself is correct and closes the previously-open Supabase schema item about `get_nearby_store_ids` having no tracked migration (moved to Fixed, above) — but the same store-approval filter wasn't applied everywhere else that reads products/stores, which this pass surfaced. **14 new findings, none previously documented:** 1 Critical (rider app — `markDelivered`/`verifyDeliveryOTP` has no server-side persistence of OTP verification, a real delivery-fraud/payout-integrity gap), 2 High (backend's legacy `POST /api/orders/create` missing the store-approval check the audited commit added elsewhere; customer app's `getProductById` missing the same check, with a confirmed order-misrouting consequence via the backend's unmatched-item fallback), 4 Medium (Supabase schema — `haversine_km` itself untracked; store-owner app — raw `fetch()` call sites bypassing the already-fixed centralized 401 handler; customer app — `getAllProducts()` unfiltered in checkout recommendations; rider app — approval checks duplicated ad hoc across two handlers, fragile but not exploitable today), 7 Low (see each area's Open Issues section above for detail). Nothing fixed in this pass — logged for a follow-up fix session, prioritized in the order listed (Critical → High → Medium → Low).
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed the Critical item):** fixed the rider app's `markDelivered`/`verifyDeliveryOTP` OTP-persistence gap found above — new `customer_orders.delivery_otp_verified_at` column (migration `20260928000000`, applied live), `verifyDeliveryOTP` now persists it on success, `markDelivered` now 400s unless it's set, and `getPickupSequence`/`loadSequence` thread the flag through so a relaunched app doesn't force re-verification. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean on `backend` and the rider app, and the new column's presence confirmed live via `information_schema.columns`. Moved from Open Issues to Fixed Issues → Rider app → Critical — **the only Critical item from today's audit; 0 Critical remain from this pass, 2 High / 4 Medium / 7 Low still open.**
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed both High items):** fixed the backend's `createOrder` missing store-approval check (added `stores!inner(is_active, is_approved)` to its product-price lookup with matching `.eq()` filters, mirroring the audited commit's own pattern — the existing "product not available at this store" error/400 mapping needed no change) and the customer app's `getProductById` missing the same check (now cross-checks against `storeService.getAllActiveProductIds()`, the existing location-independent approved-store-product-id cache already used elsewhere in the app, since this screen can be reached with no location context for the usual `nearbyIds` filter). `tsc --noEmit` clean on `backend` and the customer app. Moved both from Open Issues to Fixed Issues (Backend → High; Customer app → High). **0 Critical, 0 High remain from today's audit — 4 Medium / 7 Low still open.**
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed all 4 Medium items):** (1) Supabase schema — tracked `haversine_km` in a new migration (`20260926000000`, definition pulled verbatim from the live DB via `pg_get_functiondef`, sequenced before `20260927000000` which calls it), applied live and confirmed idempotent. (2) Store-owner app — migrated every remaining raw `fetch()` call site (`notification-inbox.tsx`, `appCache.ts`, `storeApproval.ts`, `home.tsx`) onto `apiClient`, closing the gap in the already-fixed centralized 401 handler; removed the now-dead `API_BASE` const/`config` import from `home.tsx`. (3) Customer app — `checkout.tsx`'s cold-cache fallback now passes `storeService.getAllActiveProductIds()` as `nearbyIds` to `getAllProducts()`, matching the base filter every other product path already applies. (4) Rider app — extracted a shared `getRiderApprovalState()` in `deliveryPartner.controller.ts`; `requireApprovedRider` now wraps it and `acceptOffer` now calls it directly, replacing two separate hand-rolled `delivery_partners` lookups with one. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean across `backend`, `near-now-store_owner`, and `nearandnowcustomerapp`; backend vitest 17/17 and store-owner Jest 21/21 both still passing; `expo lint` on the store-owner app shows the same pre-existing baseline, nothing new. Moved all 4 from Open Issues to Fixed Issues. **All 6 Critical/High/Medium findings from today's audit are now fixed — 7 Low items remain open.**
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed 5 of 7 remaining Low items):** while investigating the `ensure_stores_near_location` Low item (originally just "untracked function, unknown behavior"), `pg_get_functiondef` revealed it auto-generates 6 fake stores + fake products stocked at 100 units whenever fewer than 5 real stores exist near a given point — live, anon-callable, zero real call sites, clearly leftover demo/seed logic. Escalated and flagged to the user before touching production; **fixed 2026-08-04** on explicit confirmation by revoking `EXECUTE`, moved to Fixed Issues → Supabase schema → High (see there for the full writeup, including a real Postgres gotcha: revoking from `anon, authenticated` alone was a no-op because of an un-revoked `PUBLIC` grant — same latent gap likely still affects the 2026-07-23 `finalize_order_if_ready` fix, flagged for a follow-up, not fixed here). Also fixed without escalation, all straightforward: store-owner app's `notification-inbox.tsx` now rolls back optimistic mark-read state on failure (snapshot-and-revert, matching the rider/customer apps' existing pattern); rider app's `GET /delivery-partner/orders` gained opt-in `?limit`/`?offset` pagination, applied to `orders.tsx` (`?limit=50`) but deliberately *not* to `earnings.tsx` (its lifetime-total sum needs the full unbounded list — capping it would have silently under-reported real earnings, a correctness regression worse than the original issue); the website's misleadingly-named `supabaseAdmin` (anon-keyed, not service-role) was renamed to `supabaseNoSession` across all 5 importing files; the customer app's `order/confirmation/[id].tsx` got the same `getAllActiveProductIds()`-as-`nearbyIds` fix already applied to `checkout.tsx`. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean across `backend`, `frontend`, `near-now-store_owner`, `NAT_Near-Now_Rider-`, and `nearandnowcustomerapp`; backend vitest 17/17, frontend vitest 26/26, store-owner Jest 21/21 all still passing; a live anon-key `curl` RPC call against `ensure_stores_near_location` confirmed blocked (`42501`). **2 Low items remain open, both requiring a decision rather than a mechanical fix:** store-owner app's Home-tab approval-gating redundancy (needs a product call on whether Home should stay open pre-approval) and the website's `fetchProductRows` no-location-branch pagination staleness (a real fix needs snapshot isolation across the paginated scan, judged disproportionate to its low real-world impact for now).
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed the Home-tab item — product decision, not a code change):** asked the user directly whether Home should unlock pre-approval (restoring the original documented model) or stay locked (current actual behavior). **User confirmed: keep Home locked, same as every other tab** — the older "only Home accessible while pending" model is superseded, current code was already correct. No code change; moved from Open Issues to Fixed Issues → Store-owner app → High, and updated the `admin_approval_flow_fixes` project memory to stop citing the old model. **Today's audit is now fully closed except 1 Low item** (`fetchProductRows` pagination staleness — deliberately left open, judged disproportionate effort for its impact): **14 findings total, 13 fixed, 1 open by design.**
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed the last remaining item — 14/14 findings fixed):** revisited the `fetchProductRows` pagination-staleness item on request. **Fixed 2026-08-04** by resolving the eligible store-ID set as a single up-front snapshot (`getApprovedActiveStoreIds()`) instead of a live `stores` join re-evaluated on every paginated page, then reusing the location-filtered branch's existing chunked+paginated query path for both cases — the two branches now share one code path, and the no-location branch picked up real per-chunk pagination it never had before as a side benefit. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean, frontend vitest 26/26, and a live anon-key REST call confirming the new query shape works correctly against production. **Today's audit is now fully closed: 14/14 findings fixed, 0 remaining.**
- **2026-08-04 (second deep dive, same day): frontend/UI-focused re-audit of all 5 surfaces (admin panel, website, store-owner app, rider app, customer app), explicitly requested — user specifically flagged that the admin panel was "missing quite a few info," buttons "may not be working correctly," and asked for a store/rider online-offline admin toggle plus admin notifications for it.** Run as 5 parallel passes, scoped to UI/UX completeness and correctness (not backend security, already covered by the two prior passes today). Confirmed the exact gap the user described before dispatching the audits: `DeliveryPage.tsx` already had a working rider online/offline toggle, `StoresPage.tsx` only showed a read-only badge for stores. **Fixed immediately, in parallel with the audits** — see Admin panel → High, above, for the full writeup (store toggle added, `notifyAdminAction()` generalized and wired into all 4 store/rider online-offline + approve/revoke call sites, 2 new notification types). **68 additional findings logged to Open Issues** (not fixed — user asked to log for later): Admin panel 11 (0 Critical/3 High/5 Medium/3 Low — mostly `ReportsPage.tsx` fabricated fallback numbers, a decorative password field, missing store/rider info on Orders, several dead/inert controls), Website frontend 24 (2 Critical/8 High/9 Medium/5 Low — dead `/register` and "Add to Wishlist" button top the list, plus 5 dead footer links, no stock-check on Product Detail, and 3 mutually-contradicting support contact details across the site), Store-owner app 13 (1 Critical/3 High/6 Medium/3 Low — a fully fake "Send a message" support form tops the list, plus two screens unreachable after onboarding), Rider app 8 (0 Critical/3 High/4 Medium/1 Low — notifications with no error state or order deep-link, an inert-looking Vehicle Number field), Customer app 12 (0 Critical/2 High/6 Medium/4 Low — a dead `/support/help` route on the live-tracking screen, and Bill/Order Summary omitting every fee/discount line item). Full detail for each finding filed under its area's Open Issues section above, dated "2026-08-04 frontend deep dive." **Grand total across both audits today: 82 findings (14 fixed + 6 from this pass fixed inline = 20 fixed, 68 open, logged for a future prioritized fix pass — priority-order recommendation: the 3 Criticals first (website `/register` + wishlist button, store-owner fake support form), then the High-severity items, especially the ones with a direct revenue/trust angle (`ReportsPage.tsx`'s fabricated numbers, the website's 3 contradicting support contacts, store-owner's two unreachable post-onboarding screens).**
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed all 3 Admin panel High items, user-requested):** (1) `ReportsPage.tsx` — removed the `|| 198`-style fake-fallback pattern from all 5 metric tiles, replaced the permanently-static "Conv. Rate: 4.2%" with a real "Cancelled" count (no visitor-tracking system exists in this app, so a genuine conversion rate can't be computed — better to show a real metric than fake one), and made the Revenue Trend "Growing"/"Declining" badge reflect `stats.revenueGrowth`'s actual sign. (2) Admin Settings' "Current Password" field now actually does something: backend `updateAdmin` requires and `bcrypt.compare()`-verifies it for self-service password changes (a `super_admin` resetting someone *else's* password remains unchanged — that's a deliberate reset flow with no old password to prove), frontend now sends it. (3) Orders — `adminService.ts`'s `Order` type, `getOrders()`, and `getOrderById()` now carry store name(s) and assigned-rider name/phone (batch-fetched from `stores`/`delivery_partners` via the already-selected `store_orders.store_id`/new `delivery_partner_id`); `OrdersPage.tsx` gained a "Store / Rider" column, `OrderDetailPage.tsx` gained a "Fulfillment" card. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean on `backend` and `admin`, a full `vite build`, and the backend vitest suite (17/17) still passing. **Admin panel: 0 Critical/0 High remain from today's second audit — 6 Medium/1 Low (new) still open (correction: the second audit logged 6 Medium items, not 5 as originally summarized).**
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed all 6 Admin panel Medium items):** (1) `ReportsPage.tsx`'s dead "Advanced Filters" button removed (no real filter concept exists to back it beyond the already-present period selector). (2) The entire Appearance tab removed — confirmed zero i18n/timezone/theming infrastructure exists anywhere in this codebase, so every control in it (not just the 2 originally flagged) was decorative; asked the user first on the one item needing a build-vs-skip call (customer suspend, below), but this one was an unambiguous "remove the lie" case, not a feature decision. (3) Order status dropdown (`OrdersPage.tsx` + `OrderDetailPage.tsx`) now `confirm()`s before jumping to "Delivered"/"Cancelled" specifically. (4) Push "All Apps" now genuinely reaches customers too (new direct `app_users.expo_push_token` query, no RPC needed since that table has no public-read policy to carve around) — target selector also split into explicit "All/Drivers/Stores/Customers" buttons. (5) Dashboard Recent Orders/Top Products rows are now real links to their detail/edit pages. (6) Customer suspend/ban — confirmed with the user to build the real thing rather than defer: new `app_users.is_suspended` column (migration `20260930000000`, applied live), enforced in `requireCustomer` middleware (403 on every request) and at OTP login (403 before a session token is even issued), with a real toggle button + `admin_notifications` broadcast on both `CustomersPage.tsx` and `CustomerDetailPage.tsx`. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean on `backend`/`admin`, a full `vite build`, and backend vitest 17/17 still passing. **Admin panel fully closed: 0 Critical/0 High/0 Medium remain from today's second audit — only the pre-existing 1 new Low (store delete/deactivate) and the 3 older Low items remain.**
- **2026-08-04 (same day, closed the remaining 4 Admin panel Low items — all 11 findings from today's admin panel audit now resolved):** (1) `AdminHeader.tsx`'s notification-bell mark-as-read now awaits the RPC and only updates local state on success. (2) `AdminLoginPage.tsx` now redirects an already-authenticated admin away on mount, not just post-submit. (3) `QuickAddModal`'s form now resets on every open, not just after a successful submit, so Cancel/X/backdrop-click all leave it clean next time. (4) Investigated "no delete for Stores" and found it isn't actually a missing feature: `store_orders`/`store_payouts`/`delivery_partners_payouts` all `RESTRICT` on `store_id` delete (confirmed via `pg_constraint`), so a real hard-delete is impossible for any store with order history — moved to Confirmed Not Bugs rather than built, since revoke + offline already provides the only viable form of "removal." Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean and a full `vite build`. **Admin panel: 0 open items remain from either of today's two audits.**
- **2026-08-04 (same day, user pushback on the "Stores can't be deleted, confirmed not a bug" call):** user correctly pointed out that "delete" doesn't require a real row deletion — soft-delete with preserved history satisfies the actual requirement, and asked for it to match riders' existing Delete button. Investigating that existing rider delete to build the store equivalent found it had the identical `RESTRICT`-FK problem already, silently: it hard-deleted `delivery_partners` with no error check on that call, which would fail for any rider with a completed delivery (payout row) and then fail loudly on the `app_users` cascade. **Fixed 2026-08-04**: real soft-delete for both — new `deleted_at` columns on `stores`/`delivery_partners` (migration `20260930010000`), rider's `deleteDeliveryPartner` rewritten to set `status='offboarded'`/`deleted_at` instead of hard-deleting (plus a new restore endpoint), store's delete/restore added as direct writes matching that page's existing convention. Both `StoresPage.tsx`/`DeliveryPage.tsx` gained a "Deleted" filter tab and Delete/Restore buttons, with `admin_notifications` broadcasts on both actions. See Fixed Issues → Admin panel, above, for the full writeup — this supersedes and removes the "Confirmed Not Bugs" entry logged earlier today. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean on `backend`/`admin`, a full `vite build`, backend vitest 17/17, and both columns confirmed live.
- **2026-08-04 (closed the Store-owner app's sole Critical item):** fixed the fake "Send a message" support form — new `support_messages` table (generic across roles for future reuse), a real backend endpoint that persists the message and broadcasts to `admin_notifications` (same real-time-visible channel already used elsewhere in this app), and `help.tsx` now actually calls it instead of a `setTimeout`. See Fixed Issues → Store-owner app, above, for the full writeup. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean, a full `vite build` on `admin`, backend vitest 17/17, and store-owner Jest 21/21. **Store-owner app: 0 Critical remain — 3 High/6 Medium/3 Low still open.**
- **2026-08-04 (closed 1 of 2 Customer app High items):** fixed "Need help with this order?" on the live-tracking screen — was linking to `/support/help` (a route that never existed, masked by an `as any` cast) instead of the real `/settings/support`. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean (the route now typechecks without the cast, confirming it's real). **Customer app: 1 High remains** (Bill/Order Summary omitting fee/discount line items) **— 5 Medium/3 Low also still open.**
- **2026-08-04 (closed the last Customer app High item — and caught a real pricing bug in the process):** fixed Bill/Order Summary's missing fee/discount line items (client-side type/mapper gap only — the backend already returned everything via `select('*')`). While building it, discovered the customer app's checkout total calculation itself double-charged GST on the Platform+Handling fee (~₹0.75–1 overcharge per order vs. the website, for every order) — fixed by making GST an informational-only breakdown instead of an additive charge, matching the website's reference implementation and the backend's own trusted-floor logic. Also verified, per explicit request: delivery fee is ₹0 on both platforms, neither app double-charges GST on the item subtotal (already fixed historically), and both platforms already correctly redirect away from checkout when the cart empties (no code change needed for either of those three). Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean on the customer app, and the website's `tsc --noEmit` + vitest 26/26 (unaffected, checked only). **Customer app: 0 High remain — 5 Medium/3 Low still open.**
- **2026-08-04 (regression, self-inflicted): the `stores.deleted_at` column added for the store soft-delete feature (this doc, above) broke the admin panel's store list outright — "Failed to load resource: 401" / "Error fetching stores" — reported live by the user immediately after deploy.** Root cause: `stores` has explicit per-column `SELECT` grants to `anon`/`authenticated` (not a table-level grant) ever since the 2026-07-29 `expo_push_token` lockdown (`20260830000000_stores_restrict_push_token_exposure.sql`) — every column is individually granted except that one. `ALTER TABLE stores ADD COLUMN deleted_at` (migration `20260930010000`) only inherited `UPDATE`/`INSERT`/`REFERENCES`, **not** `SELECT`, confirmed via `information_schema.column_privileges` (`name`/`is_active` had `SELECT` for both roles, `deleted_at` didn't). Since Postgres denies an entire `SELECT` list if any single requested column is inaccessible, `StoresPage.tsx`'s everyday stores-list query (which explicitly lists `deleted_at`, added in the same soft-delete fix) failed outright for every admin the moment it shipped — `delivery_partners.deleted_at` was unaffected since that table uses a normal table-level grant, so only Stores broke, not Delivery. **Fixed 2026-08-04** with a one-line migration, `GRANT SELECT (deleted_at) ON public.stores TO anon, authenticated;` (`20260930030000`, applied live). Verified two ways: `information_schema.column_privileges` confirms the grant now exists, and a live anon-key `curl` replicating `StoresPage.tsx`'s exact query (all 16 columns including `deleted_at`) now returns `200` with real data, where it would have 401'd before. Confirmed via grep that no other app (customer, website, store-owner) selects `stores.deleted_at`, so nothing else was ever affected by this gap.
- **Product decision, 2026-08-04: removed the "GST charges" line from the checkout/cart/order-summary bill breakdown on the customer app entirely** — explicit request, on the reasoning that GST doesn't need to be broken out as its own line at all. `app/support/checkout.tsx`, `app/support/cart.tsx` (including its "How fees are calculated" info modal, which had its own GST section — the info icon was moved to the Delivery Fee row instead of dropped, since Platform/Handling/Delivery explanations are still there), `app/order/[id].tsx`, and `app/order/confirmation/[id].tsx` all had the row removed. The website's checkout already never showed a separate GST line to begin with (confirmed via grep — only GSTIN-for-invoice copy exists there), so no change was needed on that side; both platforms now agree exactly. Cleaned up the now-fully-unused `GST_RATE` constant and `calcFeeGst()` helper from `constants/fees.ts` entirely (confirmed via grep zero remaining references anywhere in the app) rather than leaving dead exports around — `calcOrderTotal()`'s return type dropped the `gst` field accordingly. The underlying total math is unchanged (GST was already folded into the fixed Platform/Handling fee totals as of the double-charge fix above, not added separately) — this only removes the display line, it doesn't change what's charged. Verified via `tsc --noEmit` clean on the customer app.
